Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any number_theory module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 657. The projective resolution has length at most 596 with Betti numbers bounded by 1.

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_657(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 657. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^596(S, F) is trivial. QED.
